<p dir="auto">Algorit Fast Fouriear Trasform
MATLAB OCTAVE LINUX CAD ALGORITM FAST FOURIEAR TRASFORM
LANGEiGNE C ALGORITM
MICROCONTROLLLER 8 BIT ALGORTiM ATMEL CIRCUIT IN CHIP</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-c notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="#include &lt;sys/types.h&gt;
#include &lt;sys/stat.h&gt;
#include &lt;fcntl.h&gt;
#include &lt;termios.h&gt;
#include &lt;stdio.h&gt;
#include &lt;stdlib.h&gt;
#include &lt;signal.h&gt;
#include &lt;math
#define BAUDRATE B38400
/* change this definition for the correct port */
#define MODEMDEVICE &quot;/dev/ttyS0&quot;
#define _POSIX_SOURCE 1 /* POSIX compliant source */

#define FALSE 0
#define TRUE 1

#define BUFDATOS 60*8*8
#define MUESTRAS 512
#define FSAMPLE 512
#define PI 3.14159265359
#define ADCREF 1.7
 
int fft(int i,int j,float real[],float img[]);
int dato=0;
int fftnormalizada(int datostimeadc[MUESTRAS]);
float datosrealfft[MUESTRAS];
float datosimgfft[MUESTRAS];
float datosabsfft[MUESTRAS];
int converhex(char axcharhex[2]);
//#define BUFDATOS 3360
//#define MUESTRAS 480


volatile int STOP=FALSE;
//end def interface


void * interface (void * sigdato);

//volatile sig_atomic_t okdatos=0;

typedef struct
{
  int okdatos;
}SignalInter;


pthread_t idthreadinter;
pthread_mutex_t mutexdatos;
 SignalInter signalinter;

int main(int argc, char **argv)
 {
   string sline;
   suseconds_t ms;
   double utime;
   char datosbuf[BUFDATOS];
   char *apdatosbuf=datosbuf;
   int sigdato=0;
   int pid;
   // struct sigaction act;
   //memset(&amp;act,'/0',sizeof(act));
   //act.sa_handler=&amp;interface;
   //signal(SIGINT,(void *)interface );

   //interface();
   //system(&quot;stty -F /dev/ttyS0 38400 -parenb -parodd cs8 hupcl -cstopb cread clocal -crtscts ignbrk -brkint ignpar -parmrk -inpck -istrip -inlcr -igncr -icrnl -ixon -ixoff -iuclc -ixany -imaxbel -opost -olcuc -ocrnl onlcr -onocr -onlret -ofill -ofdel nl0 cr0 tab0 bs0 vt0 ff0 -isig -icanon -iexten -echo echoe echok -echonl -noflsh -xcase -tostop -echoprt echoctl echoke start 0x79 stop 0xa &quot;);
   int j=0;
   
   /*pid=fork();
   if(pid&lt;0)
     {
       fprintf(stderr,&quot;Fork monitorV error\n&quot;);
     }
   else
     {
       if(pid==1)
	 interface(&amp;sigdato);	 
     }
   */
  
   signalinter.okdatos=0;

   pthread_mutex_init(&amp;mutexdatos,NULL);
   int error;
   error=pthread_create(&amp;idthreadinter,NULL,interface,NULL);
   if(error!=0)
     {
       perror(&quot;error arrancando thread&quot;);
       exit(-1); 
     }

   // interface(&amp;sigdato);   
   while(1)     {
     try {
       //system(&quot;cat /dev/ttyS0 | head -n 4098 &gt; /home/micro/vrmstrue/datos.txt&quot;);
       //interface(&amp;sigdato);   
       while(signalinter.okdatos==0)
	 {}
       
       pthread_mutex_lock (&amp;mutexdatos);
       signalinter.okdatos=0;
       pthread_mutex_unlock (&amp;mutexdatos);
        

        system(&quot;perl /home/micro/vrmstrue/hexa.per&quot;);

       /*
       apdatosbuf=stpcpy(apdatosbuf,&quot;a&quot;);
       if(j==10)
	 {
	   puts(datosbuf);
	   printf(&quot;Datos: %s&quot;,datosbuf);
	   free(apdatosbuf);
	   apdatosbuf=datosbuf;
	 }
       j++;
       */
    

       FILE *fplot;
       fplot= popen(GNUPLOT_PATH,&quot;w&quot;);
       fputs(&quot;set term png \n&quot;,fplot);
       fputs(&quot;set output \&quot;vrms.png\&quot; \n&quot;,fplot);
       fputs( &quot;set autoscale \n&quot;,fplot);
       //fprintf(gp, &quot;plot abs(x*sin(sqrt(abs(x))))+400&quot;);
       fputs(&quot;set xlabel \&quot;tiempo 1 [seg], muestra 1.953125E-3 [seg] \&quot; \n&quot;,fplot);
       // fputs(&quot;set format x \&quot;%03.0f \&quot; \n&quot;,fplot);
       fputs(&quot;set xrange [1:512] \n&quot;,fplot);
       fputs(&quot;set ylabel \&quot;Voltaje [R.M.S]\&quot; \n&quot;,fplot);
       //fputs(&quot;set format y \&quot;%03.1f \&quot; \n&quot;,fplot);
       
       fputs(&quot;plot \&quot;datosp.txt\&quot; with lines title 'Monitoreo de Voltaje' &quot;,fplot);
       pclose(fplot);

       system(&quot;cp vrms.png  valores.txt /var/www/&quot;);
       puts(&quot;nueva grafica&quot;);

     }
     catch(std::bad_alloc&amp;)
       {
	 cout &lt;&lt; &quot;error I/O Memoria&quot;&lt;&lt; endl;
	 pthread_exit(NULL);
       }
   }
   return 0;
 }


int converthex(char axcharhex[255])
{
  int dato1=0,dato2=0,dato3=0,datohex=0;
  char char1=axcharhex[0];
    char char2=axcharhex[1];  
    char char3=axcharhex[2];  
    //  char1='a';
    //char2='b';
    //char3='f';

switch(char1)
   {
  
   case 'a':{ dato1=10;
              break;}
   case 'b': {dato1=11;
	       break;} 
   case 'c': {dato1=12;
       break;}
   case 'd': {dato1=13;break;}
   case 'e': {dato1=14;break;}
   case 'f': {dato1=15;break;}
   case '0': {dato1=0;break;}
   case '1': {dato1=1;break;}
   case '2': {dato1=2;break;}
   case '3': {dato1=3;break;}
   case '4': {dato1=4;break;}
   case '5': {dato1=5;break;}
   case '6': {dato1=6;break;}
   case '7': {dato1=7;break;}
   case '8': {dato1=8;break;}
   case '9': {dato1=9;break;}
   default :{dato1=0;break;}
  }

   switch(char2)
   {
   case 'a': {dato2=10;break;}
   case 'b': {dato2=11;break;} 
   case 'c': {dato2=12;break;}
   case 'd': {dato2=13;break;}
   case 'e': {dato2=14;break;}
   case 'f': {dato2=15;break;}
   case '0': {dato2=0;break;}
   case '1': {dato2=1;break;}
   case '2': {dato2=2;break;}
   case '3': {dato2=3;break;}
   case '4': {dato2=4;break;}
   case '5': {dato2=5;break;}
   case '6': {dato2=6;break;}
   case '7': {dato2=7;break;}
   case '8': {dato2=8;break;}
   case '9': {dato2=9;break;}
   default : {dato2=0;break;}
  }  
  
  switch(char3)
   {
   case 'a': {dato3=10;break;}
   case 'b': {dato3=11;break;} 
   case 'c': {dato3=12;break;}
   case 'd': {dato3=13;break;}
   case 'e': {dato3=14;break;}
   case 'f': {dato3=15;break;}
   case '0': {dato3=0;break;}
   case '1': {dato3=1;break;}
   case '2': {dato3=2;break;}
   case '3': {dato3=3;break;}
   case '4': {dato3=4;break;}
   case '5': {dato3=5;break;}
   case '6': {dato3=6;break;}
   case '7': {dato3=7;break;}
   case '8': {dato3=8;break;}
   case '9': {dato3=9;break;}
   default : {dato3=0;break;}
  }  
  
  datohex=(dato1&lt;&lt;8)+(dato2&lt;&lt;4)+(dato3&lt;&lt;0);
  //datohex=(dato2&lt;&lt;4);
  //printf(&quot;%s %c %c %c %d %d %d %d\n&quot;,axcharhex,char1,char2,char3,dato1,dato2,dato3,datohex);
//dato1=5;
    // printf(&quot;dato1 %c %d &quot;,char1,dato1);     
  return datohex;
}

void * interface(void * sigdato)
{
  int fd,c, res;
  struct termios oldtio,newtio;
  FILE *fdatosinter;
  FILE *fdatosfft;
  size_t len=0;
  char datosbuf[BUFDATOS];
  char *apdatosbuf=datosbuf;
  char *apax=datosbuf;
  char buf[255];
  int datosadc[MUESTRAS];
  char datosbuffft[BUFDATOS];

  //apdatosbuf=(char *)malloc(sizeof(char)*100);
  //apdatosbuf=&amp;datosbuf;
  /*
 Open modem device for reading and writing and not as controlling tty
     because we don't want to get killed if linenoise sends CTRL-C.
  */
  fd = open(MODEMDEVICE, O_RDWR | O_NOCTTY );
  if (fd &lt;0) {perror(MODEMDEVICE); exit(-1); }

  tcgetattr(fd,&amp;oldtio); /* save current serial port settings */
  bzero(&amp;newtio, sizeof(newtio)); /* clear struct for new port settings */

  /*
     BAUDRATE: Set bps rate. You could also use cfsetispeed and cfsetospeed.
     CRTSCTS : output hardware flow control (only used if the cable has
     all necessary lines. See sect. 7 of Serial-HOWTO)
     CS8     : 8n1 (8bit,no parity,1 stopbit)
     CLOCAL  : local connection, no modem contol
     CREAD   : enable receiving characters
  */
  //  newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CRTSCTS | CS8 | CLOCAL | CREAD | ICRNL |PARODD;
  //newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CS8 | CLOCAL | CREAD ;
  newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CS8 | CREAD|CLOCAL| IGNPAR;
  //newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CS8 | CREAD | CLOCAL  
  //  IGNPAR  : ignore bytes with parity errors
  //   ICRNL   : map CR to NL (otherwise a CR input on the other computer
  //  will not terminate input)
  //  otherwise make device raw (no other input processing)
 
  //newtio.c_iflag = IGNPAR | ICRNL;

  //newtio.c_iflag = PARODD ;
  newtio.c_iflag = PARODD |IGNPAR;
  /*
    Raw output.
  */
  newtio.c_oflag = 0;
 newtio.c_iflag = 0;
  //newtio.c_iflag = ~OPOST;
  /*
    ICANON  : enable canonical input
    disable all echo functionality, and don't send signals to calling program
  */
   newtio.c_lflag = ICANON;
  // newtio.c_lflag = 0;
  /*
    initialize all control characters
     default values can be found in /usr/include/termios.h, and are given
     in the comments, but we don't need them here
  */
  newtio.c_cc[VINTR]    = 0;     /* Ctrl-c */
  newtio.c_cc[VQUIT]    = 0;     /* Ctrl-\ */
  newtio.c_cc[VERASE]   = 0;     /* del */
  newtio.c_cc[VKILL]    = 0;     /* @ */
  newtio.c_cc[VEOF]     = 4;     /* Ctrl-d */
  newtio.c_cc[VTIME]    = 0;     /* inter-character timer unused */
  newtio.c_cc[VMIN]     = 1;     /* blocking read until 1 character arrives */
  newtio.c_cc[VSWTC]    = 0;     /* '\0' */
  newtio.c_cc[VSTART]   = 0;     /* Ctrl-q */
  newtio.c_cc[VSTOP]    = 0;     /* Ctrl-s */
  newtio.c_cc[VSUSP]    = 0;     /* Ctrl-z */
  newtio.c_cc[VEOL]     = 0;     /* '\0' */
  newtio.c_cc[VREPRINT] = 0;     /* Ctrl-r */
  newtio.c_cc[VDISCARD] = 0;     /* Ctrl-u */
  newtio.c_cc[VWERASE]  = 0;     /* Ctrl-w */
  newtio.c_cc[VLNEXT]   = 0;     /* Ctrl-v */
  newtio.c_cc[VEOL2]    = 0;     /* '\0' */

  /*
    now clean the modem line and activate the settings for the port
  */
  tcflush(fd, TCIFLUSH);
  tcsetattr(fd,TCSANOW,&amp;newtio);

  /*
    terminal settings done, now handle input
    In this example, inputting a 'z' at the beginning of a line will
    exit the program.
  */
  int j=0;
  int i=0;
  int muestra=0;
  int dirapdatos = *apdatosbuf;
  char axchar[255];
  int datorx=0;
  while (STOP==FALSE)
    {
      datorx=0;
     /* loop until we have a terminating condition */
    /* read blocks program execution until a line terminating character is
       input, even if more than 255 chars are input. If the number
       of characters read is smaller than the number of chars available,
       subsequent reads will return the remaining chars. res will be set
       to the actual number of characters actually read */
      res = read(fd,buf,255);
      buf[res]=0;

      strcpy(axchar,buf);
      //axchar[0]=(char)buf[0];
      //axchar[1]=(char)buf[1];
      //axchar[2]=(char)buf[2];

       datorx=converthex(axchar);
       //puts(buf);
      datosadc[j]=datorx;
      // printf(&quot;%s  %d \n&quot;,buf,datorx);
      // apdatosbuf=stpcpy(apdatosbuf,buf);
      strcat(datosbuf,buf);

   //puts(datosbuf);
    //memcpy(datosbuf,buf,strlen(buf));
    //buf[res]=0;             /* set end of string, so we can printf */
    //printf(&quot;:%s,%d\n&quot;, buf, res);
    //printf(&quot;transimosion :%d %s \n&quot;, j,buf);
    j++;
    if(j==MUESTRAS)
      {
        j=0;	


        remove (&quot;datos.txt&quot;);
        fdatosinter=fopen(&quot;datos.txt&quot;,&quot;w+&quot;);
        if(fdatosinter==NULL)
          {
	    fclose(fdatosinter);
	    puts(&quot;error leyecdo archivo datos&quot;);
          }
        else
          {



	    //puts(datosbuf);
                len=strlen(datosbuf);
                fwrite(datosbuf,len,1,fdatosinter);
                //memset(datosbuf,0,BUFDATOS);
                //fflush(*apdatosbuf);
                //apdatosbuf=memset(apdatosbuf,0,BUFDATOS);
                //apdatosbuf=strcpy(apdatosbuf,&quot;&quot;);


              

                //apdatosbuf=NULL;
                 //*apdatosbuf=NULL;
                //apdatosbuf=&amp;datosbuf;
                //apdatosbuf=datosbuf;
                //apdatosbuf=strcpy(apdatosbuf,&quot;&quot;);
                //puts(datosbuf);
                // apdatosbuf=datosbuf;
	


          }
        fclose(fdatosinter);
	strcpy(datosbuf,&quot;&quot;);
	//*apdatosbuf=NULL;
	//free(apdatosbuf);
	//apdatosbuf=(char *)malloc(sizeof(char)*BUFDATOS);

        pthread_mutex_lock (&amp;mutexdatos);
        signalinter.okdatos=1;
	pthread_mutex_unlock (&amp;mutexdatos);
  

	fftnormalizada(datosadc);

	for(i=0;i&lt;MUESTRAS;i++)
	  {
	    len=sprintf(axchar,&quot;%f\n&quot;,datosabsfft[i]);
	    strcat(datosbuffft,axchar);
	  }
	//puts(datosbuffft);


        
	remove (&quot;datosfft.txt&quot;);
	fdatosfft=fopen(&quot;datosfft.txt&quot;,&quot;w+&quot;);
	if(fdatosfft==NULL)
          {
	    fclose(fdatosfft);
	    puts(&quot;error leyecdo archivo datos&quot;);
          }
        else
          {
	    
	     len=strlen(datosbuffft);
	     fwrite(datosbuffft,len,1,fdatosfft);

          }
        fclose(fdatosfft);
	strcpy(datosbuffft,&quot;&quot;);


	//        pthread_mutex_lock (&amp;mutexdatos);
        //signalinter.okdatos=1;
	//pthread_mutex_unlock (&amp;mutexdatos);



      }
    if (buf[0]=='z')
      STOP=TRUE;
  }
  /* restore the old port settings */
  tcsetattr(fd,TCSANOW,&amp;oldtio);
  //exit(sigdato);
}


//** function fft
// i : 1 transformada inversa, :-1 trasnformada
// j : NMUESTRAS
// real : argumento real
// img: argumento imaginario
int fft(int i, int j, float real[], float img[])
{

float f = sqrt(1.0F / (float)j);
int i1;
int k;
for( k = i1 = 0; k &lt; j; k++)

{

if(i1 &gt;= k)
{
float f1 = (real[i1]) * f;
float f2 = (img[i1]) * f;
real[i1] = (real[k]) * f;
img[i1] = (img[k]) * f;
real[k] = f1;
img[k] = f2;
}

int k1;
for(k1 = j / 2; k1 &gt;= 1 &amp;&amp; i1 &gt;= k1; k1 /= 2)
i1 -= k1;


i1 += k1;
}


int l1 = 1;
int i2;

for( i2 = 2 * l1; l1 &lt; j; i2 = 2 * l1)
{

float f3 = ((float)i * 3.141593F) / (float)l1;
int j2;
for( j2 = 0; j2 &lt; l1; j2++)
{
float f4 = (float)j2 * f3;
float f5 = (float)cos(f4);
float f6 = (float)sin(f4);
int l;
for( l = j2; l &lt; j; l += i2)
{
int j1 = l + l1;
float f7 = f5 * (real[j1]) - f6 * (img[j1]);
float f8 = f5 * (img[j1]) + f6 * (real[j1]);
real[j1] = real[l] - f7;
img[j1] = img[l] - f8;
real[l] += f7;
img[l] += f8;
}

}

l1 = i2;
l1 = i2;


}

return 1;
}
//**********end FFT **************


int fftnormalizada(int datostimeadc[MUESTRAS])
{
int var,len,k;
char outstring;
char buferr[10];
float promedio,M,prompos;
float w[MUESTRAS];
float f[MUESTRAS]; 

 for(k=0;k&lt;MUESTRAS;k++)
      datostimeadc[k]=1*sin((2*3.1415927*k*60/512));


 for(k = 0; k &lt; MUESTRAS; k++)
   f[k] = k*FSAMPLE/(MUESTRAS);
 for(k = 0 ;k&lt; MUESTRAS; k++)
   w[k] = 2*PI*f[k];
 
 
     
     
     k=0;
     promedio=0;
     while( k&lt;MUESTRAS)
       {
	 // al=(2*(al-am)/(aM-am))-1;
	 //al=al*100;
	 datosrealfft[k] =datostimeadc[k], promedio += datostimeadc[k] ;
	 k++;
       }
     M=k;
     
     promedio /= M;
     for(k = 0; k&lt;M; k++){
       datosrealfft[k] -= promedio;
      
     }

     //FFT 
//     fft(-1,MUESTRAS/2,datosrealfft,datosimgfft);
 fft(-1,MUESTRAS,datosrealfft,datosimgfft);
 for(k=0;k&lt;MUESTRAS;k++)
   {
    
     //datosabsfft[k]=sqrt (datosrealfft[k]*datosrealfft[k]/MUESTRAS+datosimgfft[k]*datosimgfft[k]/MUESTRAS);
    
     datosabsfft[k]= 2*sqrt(datosrealfft[k]*datosrealfft[k]/(MUESTRAS*MUESTRAS)+datosimgfft[k]*datosimgfft[k]/(MUESTRAS*MUESTRAS));
   }

        
 return 1;
}

<span class="pl-k">#include</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-s1">math</span>

<span class="pl-k">#define</span> <span class="pl-c1">TRUE</span> 1

<span class="pl-k">#define</span> <span class="pl-c1">FSAMPLE</span> 512

<span class="pl-k">#define</span> <span class="pl-c1">ADCREF</span> 1.7
 
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">fft</span>(<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">i</span>,<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span>,<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">real</span>[],<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">img</span>[]);
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">dato</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">fftnormalizada</span>(<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">datostimeadc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>]);
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosrealfft</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>];
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosimgfft</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>];
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosabsfft</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>];
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">converhex</span>(<span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">axcharhex</span>[<span class="pl-c1">2</span>]);

<span class="pl-c">//#define MUESTRAS 480</span>

<span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-en">interface</span> (<span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">sigdato</span>);

<span class="pl-k">typedef</span> <span class="pl-k">struct</span>
{

}<span class="pl-smi">SignalInter</span>;


<span class="pl-smi">pthread_t</span> <span class="pl-s1">idthreadinter</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">pthread_mutex_t</span> <span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>;
 <span class="pl-smi">SignalInter</span> <span class="pl-s1">signalinter</span>;

 {

   <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">apdatosbuf</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>;
   <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">sigdato</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;

   <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
   
   <span class="pl-c">/*pid=fork();</span>
<span class="pl-c">   if(pid&lt;0)</span>
<span class="pl-c">     {</span>
<span class="pl-c">       fprintf(stderr,"Fork monitorV error\n");</span>
<span class="pl-c">     }</span>
<span class="pl-c">   else</span>
<span class="pl-c">     {</span>

<span class="pl-c">	 interface(&amp;sigdato);	 </span>
<span class="pl-c">     }</span>
<span class="pl-c">   */</span>
  
   <span class="pl-s1">signalinter</span>.<span class="pl-c1">okdatos</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;

   <span class="pl-en">pthread_mutex_init</span>(<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>,<span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>);
   <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">error</span>;
   <span class="pl-s1">error</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">pthread_create</span>(<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">idthreadinter</span>,<span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>,<span class="pl-s1">interface</span>,<span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>);
   <span class="pl-k">if</span>(<span class="pl-s1">error</span><span class="pl-c1">!=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>)
     {
       <span class="pl-en">perror</span>(<span class="pl-s">"error arrancando thread"</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">exit</span>(<span class="pl-c1">-1</span>); 
     }

   <span class="pl-c">// interface(&amp;sigdato);   </span>
   <span class="pl-k">while</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>)     {
     <span class="pl-s1">try</span> {
       <span class="pl-c">//system("cat /dev/ttyS0 | head -n 4098 &gt; /home/micro/vrmstrue/datos.txt");</span>
       <span class="pl-c">//interface(&amp;sigdato);   </span>
       <span class="pl-k">while</span>(<span class="pl-s1">signalinter</span>.<span class="pl-c1">okdatos</span><span class="pl-c1">==</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>)
	 {}
       
       <span class="pl-en">pthread_mutex_lock</span> (<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>);
       <span class="pl-s1">signalinter</span>.<span class="pl-c1">okdatos</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
       <span class="pl-en">pthread_mutex_unlock</span> (<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>);
        

        <span class="pl-en">system</span>(<span class="pl-s">"perl /home/micro/vrmstrue/hexa.per"</span>);

       <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">       apdatosbuf=stpcpy(apdatosbuf,"a");</span>
<span class="pl-c">       if(j==10)</span>

<span class="pl-c">	   printf("Datos: %s",datosbuf);</span>
<span class="pl-c">	   free(apdatosbuf);</span>
<span class="pl-c">	   apdatosbuf=datosbuf;</span>
<span class="pl-c">	 }</span>
<span class="pl-c">       j++;</span>
<span class="pl-c">       */</span>
    

       <span class="pl-smi">FILE</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>;
       <span class="pl-s1">fplot</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">popen</span>(<span class="pl-c1">GNUPLOT_PATH</span>,<span class="pl-s">"w"</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"set term png \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"set output \"vrms.png\" \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>( <span class="pl-s">"set autoscale \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-c">//fprintf(gp, "plot abs(x*sin(sqrt(abs(x))))+400");</span>
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"set xlabel \"tiempo 1 [seg], muestra 1.953125E-3 [seg] \" \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-c">// fputs("set format x \"%03.0f \" \n",fplot);</span>
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"set xrange [1:512] \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"set ylabel \"Voltaje [R.M.S]\" \n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-c">//fputs("set format y \"%03.1f \" \n",fplot);</span>
       
       <span class="pl-en">fputs</span>(<span class="pl-s">"plot \"datosp.txt\" with lines title 'Monitoreo de Voltaje' "</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">pclose</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fplot</span>);

       <span class="pl-en">system</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cp vrms.png  valores.txt /var/www/"</span>);
       <span class="pl-en">puts</span>(<span class="pl-s">"nueva grafica"</span>);

     }
     <span class="pl-ent">catch</span>(<span class="pl-smi">std</span>::<span class="pl-s1">bad_alloc</span><span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span>)
       {
	 <span class="pl-s1">cout</span> &lt;&lt; <span class="pl-s">"error I/O Memoria"</span>&lt;&lt; <span class="pl-s1">endl</span>;
	 <span class="pl-en">pthread_exit</span>(<span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>);
       }
   }
   <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
 }


<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">converthex</span>(<span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">axcharhex</span>[<span class="pl-c1">255</span>])
{
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>,<span class="pl-s1">datohex</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">char1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">axcharhex</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>];
    <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">char2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">axcharhex</span>[<span class="pl-c1">1</span>];  
    <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">char3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">axcharhex</span>[<span class="pl-c1">2</span>];  
    <span class="pl-c">//  char1='a';</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//char2='b';</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//char3='f';</span>

<span class="pl-k">switch</span>(<span class="pl-s1">char1</span>)
   {
  
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'a'</span>:{ <span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">10</span>;
              <span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'b'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">11</span>;
	       <span class="pl-k">break</span>;} 
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'c'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">12</span>;
       <span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'d'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">13</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'e'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">14</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'f'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">15</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'0'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'1'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'2'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">2</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'3'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'4'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">4</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'5'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'6'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">6</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'7'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">7</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'8'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">8</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'9'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">9</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">default</span> :{<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
  }

   <span class="pl-k">switch</span>(<span class="pl-s1">char2</span>)
   {
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'a'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">10</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'b'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">11</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;} 
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'c'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">12</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'d'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">13</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'e'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">14</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'f'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">15</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'0'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'1'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'2'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">2</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'3'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'4'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">4</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'5'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'6'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">6</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'7'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">7</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'8'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">8</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'9'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">9</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">default</span> : {<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
  }  
  
  <span class="pl-k">switch</span>(<span class="pl-s1">char3</span>)
   {
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'a'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">10</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'b'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">11</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;} 
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'c'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">12</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'d'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">13</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'e'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">14</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'f'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">15</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'0'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'1'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'2'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">2</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'3'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'4'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">4</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'5'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'6'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">6</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'7'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">7</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'8'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">8</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">case</span> <span class="pl-c1">'9'</span>: {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">9</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
   <span class="pl-k">default</span> : {<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-k">break</span>;}
  }  
  
  <span class="pl-s1">datohex</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>(<span class="pl-s1">dato1</span>&lt;&lt;<span class="pl-c1">8</span>)<span class="pl-c1">+</span>(<span class="pl-s1">dato2</span>&lt;&lt;<span class="pl-c1">4</span>)<span class="pl-c1">+</span>(<span class="pl-s1">dato3</span>&lt;&lt;<span class="pl-c1">0</span>);
  <span class="pl-c">//datohex=(dato2&lt;&lt;4);</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//printf("%s %c %c %c %d %d %d %d\n",axcharhex,char1,char2,char3,dato1,dato2,dato3,datohex);</span>
<span class="pl-c">//dato1=5;</span>
    <span class="pl-c">// printf("dato1 %c %d ",char1,dato1);     </span>
  <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">datohex</span>;
}

<span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-en">interface</span>(<span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">sigdato</span>)
{
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">fd</span>,<span class="pl-s1">c</span>, <span class="pl-s1">res</span>;
  <span class="pl-k">struct</span> <span class="pl-smi">termios</span> <span class="pl-s1">oldtio</span>,<span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">FILE</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">FILE</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">size_t</span> <span class="pl-s1">len</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>[<span class="pl-c1">BUFDATOS</span>];
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">apdatosbuf</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">apax</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">buf</span>[<span class="pl-c1">255</span>];
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosadc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>];
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">datosbuffft</span>[<span class="pl-c1">BUFDATOS</span>];

  <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=(char *)malloc(sizeof(char)*100);</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=&amp;datosbuf;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c"> Open modem device for reading and writing and not as controlling tty</span>
<span class="pl-c">     because we don't want to get killed if linenoise sends CTRL-C.</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">fd</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">open</span>(<span class="pl-c1">MODEMDEVICE</span>, <span class="pl-c1">O_RDWR</span> | <span class="pl-c1">O_NOCTTY</span> );
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> (<span class="pl-s1">fd</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>) {<span class="pl-en">perror</span>(<span class="pl-c1">MODEMDEVICE</span>); <span class="pl-en">exit</span>(<span class="pl-c1">-1</span>); }

  <span class="pl-en">tcgetattr</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fd</span>,<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">oldtio</span>); <span class="pl-c">/* save current serial port settings */</span>
  <span class="pl-en">bzero</span>(<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>, <span class="pl-k">sizeof</span>(<span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>)); <span class="pl-c">/* clear struct for new port settings */</span>

  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">     BAUDRATE: Set bps rate. You could also use cfsetispeed and cfsetospeed.</span>
<span class="pl-c">     CRTSCTS : output hardware flow control (only used if the cable has</span>
<span class="pl-c">     all necessary lines. See sect. 7 of Serial-HOWTO)</span>
<span class="pl-c">     CS8     : 8n1 (8bit,no parity,1 stopbit)</span>
<span class="pl-c">     CLOCAL  : local connection, no modem contol</span>
<span class="pl-c">     CREAD   : enable receiving characters</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//  newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CRTSCTS | CS8 | CLOCAL | CREAD | ICRNL |PARODD;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CS8 | CLOCAL | CREAD ;</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cflag</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">BAUDRATE</span> | <span class="pl-c1">CS8</span> | <span class="pl-c1">CREAD</span>|<span class="pl-c1">CLOCAL</span>| <span class="pl-c1">IGNPAR</span>;
  <span class="pl-c">//newtio.c_cflag = BAUDRATE | CS8 | CREAD | CLOCAL  </span>
  <span class="pl-c">//  IGNPAR  : ignore bytes with parity errors</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//   ICRNL   : map CR to NL (otherwise a CR input on the other computer</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//  will not terminate input)</span>
  <span class="pl-c">//  otherwise make device raw (no other input processing)</span>
 
  <span class="pl-c">//newtio.c_iflag = IGNPAR | ICRNL;</span>

  <span class="pl-c">//newtio.c_iflag = PARODD ;</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_iflag</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">PARODD</span> |<span class="pl-c1">IGNPAR</span>;
  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">    Raw output.</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_oflag</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
 <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_iflag</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-c">//newtio.c_iflag = ~OPOST;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">    ICANON  : enable canonical input</span>
<span class="pl-c">    disable all echo functionality, and don't send signals to calling program</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
   <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_lflag</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">ICANON</span>;
  <span class="pl-c">// newtio.c_lflag = 0;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">    initialize all control characters</span>
<span class="pl-c">     default values can be found in /usr/include/termios.h, and are given</span>
<span class="pl-c">     in the comments, but we don't need them here</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VINTR</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-c */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VQUIT</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-\ */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VERASE</span>]   <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* del */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VKILL</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* @ */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VEOF</span>]     <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">4</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-d */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VTIME</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* inter-character timer unused */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VMIN</span>]     <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* blocking read until 1 character arrives */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VSWTC</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* '\0' */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VSTART</span>]   <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-q */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VSTOP</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-s */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VSUSP</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-z */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VEOL</span>]     <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* '\0' */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VREPRINT</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-r */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VDISCARD</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-u */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VWERASE</span>]  <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-w */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VLNEXT</span>]   <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* Ctrl-v */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>.<span class="pl-c1">c_cc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">VEOL2</span>]    <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>;     <span class="pl-c">/* '\0' */</span>

  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">    now clean the modem line and activate the settings for the port</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-en">tcflush</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fd</span>, <span class="pl-c1">TCIFLUSH</span>);
  <span class="pl-en">tcsetattr</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fd</span>,<span class="pl-c1">TCSANOW</span>,<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">newtio</span>);

  <span class="pl-c">/*</span>
<span class="pl-c">    terminal settings done, now handle input</span>
<span class="pl-c">    In this example, inputting a 'z' at the beginning of a line will</span>
<span class="pl-c">    exit the program.</span>
<span class="pl-c">  */</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">i</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">muestra</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">dirapdatos</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">apdatosbuf</span>;
  <span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">axchar</span>[<span class="pl-c1">255</span>];
  <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">datorx</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
  <span class="pl-k">while</span> (<span class="pl-c1">STOP</span><span class="pl-c1">==</span>FALSE)
    {
      <span class="pl-s1">datorx</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
     <span class="pl-c">/* loop until we have a terminating condition */</span>
    <span class="pl-c">/* read blocks program execution until a line terminating character is</span>
<span class="pl-c">       input, even if more than 255 chars are input. If the number</span>
<span class="pl-c">       of characters read is smaller than the number of chars available,</span>
<span class="pl-c">       subsequent reads will return the remaining chars. res will be set</span>
<span class="pl-c">       to the actual number of characters actually read */</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">res</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">read</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fd</span>,<span class="pl-s1">buf</span>,<span class="pl-c1">255</span>);
      <span class="pl-s1">buf</span>[<span class="pl-s1">res</span>]<span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;

      <span class="pl-en">strcpy</span>(<span class="pl-s1">axchar</span>,<span class="pl-s1">buf</span>);
      <span class="pl-c">//axchar[0]=(char)buf[0];</span>
      <span class="pl-c">//axchar[1]=(char)buf[1];</span>
      <span class="pl-c">//axchar[2]=(char)buf[2];</span>

       <span class="pl-s1">datorx</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">converthex</span>(<span class="pl-s1">axchar</span>);
       <span class="pl-c">//puts(buf);</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">datosadc</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j</span>]<span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">datorx</span>;
      <span class="pl-c">// printf("%s  %d \n",buf,datorx);</span>
      <span class="pl-c">// apdatosbuf=stpcpy(apdatosbuf,buf);</span>
      <span class="pl-en">strcat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>,<span class="pl-s1">buf</span>);

   <span class="pl-c">//puts(datosbuf);</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//memcpy(datosbuf,buf,strlen(buf));</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//buf[res]=0;             /* set end of string, so we can printf */</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//printf(":%s,%d\n", buf, res);</span>
    <span class="pl-c">//printf("transimosion :%d %s \n", j,buf);</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">j</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>;
    <span class="pl-k">if</span>(<span class="pl-s1">j</span><span class="pl-c1">==</span><span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>)
      {
        <span class="pl-s1">j</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;	


        <span class="pl-en">remove</span> (<span class="pl-s">"datos.txt"</span>);
        <span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">fopen</span>(<span class="pl-s">"datos.txt"</span>,<span class="pl-s">"w+"</span>);
        <span class="pl-k">if</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span><span class="pl-c1">==</span><span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>)
          {
	    <span class="pl-en">fclose</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span>);
	    <span class="pl-en">puts</span>(<span class="pl-s">"error leyecdo archivo datos"</span>);
          }
        <span class="pl-k">else</span>
          {



	    <span class="pl-c">//puts(datosbuf);</span>
                <span class="pl-s1">len</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">strlen</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>);
                <span class="pl-en">fwrite</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>,<span class="pl-s1">len</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span>);
                <span class="pl-c">//memset(datosbuf,0,BUFDATOS);</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//fflush(*apdatosbuf);</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=memset(apdatosbuf,0,BUFDATOS);</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=strcpy(apdatosbuf,"");</span>


              

                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=NULL;</span>
                 <span class="pl-c">//*apdatosbuf=NULL;</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=&amp;datosbuf;</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=datosbuf;</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=strcpy(apdatosbuf,"");</span>
                <span class="pl-c">//puts(datosbuf);</span>
                <span class="pl-c">// apdatosbuf=datosbuf;</span>
	


          }
        <span class="pl-en">fclose</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosinter</span>);
	<span class="pl-en">strcpy</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuf</span>,<span class="pl-s">""</span>);
	<span class="pl-c">//*apdatosbuf=NULL;</span>
	<span class="pl-c">//free(apdatosbuf);</span>
	<span class="pl-c">//apdatosbuf=(char *)malloc(sizeof(char)*BUFDATOS);</span>

        <span class="pl-en">pthread_mutex_lock</span> (<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>);
        <span class="pl-s1">signalinter</span>.<span class="pl-c1">okdatos</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>;
	<span class="pl-en">pthread_mutex_unlock</span> (<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">mutexdatos</span>);
  

	<span class="pl-en">fftnormalizada</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosadc</span>);

	<span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">i</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-s1">i</span><span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>;<span class="pl-s1">i</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)
	  {
	    <span class="pl-s1">len</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">sprintf</span>(<span class="pl-s1">axchar</span>,<span class="pl-s">"%f\n"</span>,<span class="pl-s1">datosabsfft</span>[<span class="pl-s1">i</span>]);
	    <span class="pl-en">strcat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuffft</span>,<span class="pl-s1">axchar</span>);
	  }
	<span class="pl-c">//puts(datosbuffft);</span>


        
	<span class="pl-en">remove</span> (<span class="pl-s">"datosfft.txt"</span>);
	<span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">fopen</span>(<span class="pl-s">"datosfft.txt"</span>,<span class="pl-s">"w+"</span>);
	<span class="pl-k">if</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span><span class="pl-c1">==</span><span class="pl-c1">NULL</span>)
          {
	    <span class="pl-en">fclose</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span>);
	    <span class="pl-en">puts</span>(<span class="pl-s">"error leyecdo archivo datos"</span>);
          }
        <span class="pl-k">else</span>
          {
	    
	     <span class="pl-s1">len</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-en">strlen</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuffft</span>);
	     <span class="pl-en">fwrite</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuffft</span>,<span class="pl-s1">len</span>,<span class="pl-c1">1</span>,<span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span>);

          }
        <span class="pl-en">fclose</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fdatosfft</span>);
	<span class="pl-en">strcpy</span>(<span class="pl-s1">datosbuffft</span>,<span class="pl-s">""</span>);


	<span class="pl-c">//        pthread_mutex_lock (&amp;mutexdatos);</span>
        <span class="pl-c">//signalinter.okdatos=1;</span>
	<span class="pl-c">//pthread_mutex_unlock (&amp;mutexdatos);</span>



      }
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> (<span class="pl-s1">buf</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>]<span class="pl-c1">==</span><span class="pl-c1">'z'</span>)
      <span class="pl-c1">STOP</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>TRUE;
  }
  <span class="pl-c">/* restore the old port settings */</span>
  <span class="pl-en">tcsetattr</span>(<span class="pl-s1">fd</span>,<span class="pl-c1">TCSANOW</span>,<span class="pl-c1">&amp;</span><span class="pl-s1">oldtio</span>);
  <span class="pl-c">//exit(sigdato);</span>
}


<span class="pl-c">//** function fft</span>
<span class="pl-c">// i : 1 transformada inversa, :-1 trasnformada</span>
<span class="pl-c">// j : NMUESTRAS</span>
<span class="pl-c">// real : argumento real</span>
<span class="pl-c">// img: argumento imaginario</span>
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">fft</span>(<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">i</span>, <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span>, <span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">real</span>[], <span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">img</span>[])
{

<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">sqrt</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1.0F</span> / (<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-s1">j</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">i1</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">k</span>;
<span class="pl-k">for</span>( <span class="pl-s1">k</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">i1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)

{

<span class="pl-k">if</span>(<span class="pl-s1">i1</span> &gt;= <span class="pl-s1">k</span>)
{
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">i1</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">i1</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">i1</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">i1</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f1</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f2</span>;
}

<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">k1</span>;
<span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span> / <span class="pl-c1">2</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k1</span> &gt;= <span class="pl-c1">1</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span> <span class="pl-s1">i1</span> &gt;= <span class="pl-s1">k1</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k1</span> /= <span class="pl-c1">2</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">i1</span> <span class="pl-c1">-=</span> <span class="pl-s1">k1</span>;


<span class="pl-s1">i1</span> <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">k1</span>;
}


<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">l1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">i2</span>;

<span class="pl-k">for</span>( <span class="pl-s1">i2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">l1</span>; <span class="pl-s1">l1</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span>; <span class="pl-s1">i2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">l1</span>)
{

<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f3</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> ((<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-s1">i</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-c1">3.141593F</span>) / (<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-s1">l1</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j2</span>;
<span class="pl-k">for</span>( <span class="pl-s1">j2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>; <span class="pl-s1">j2</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-s1">l1</span>; <span class="pl-s1">j2</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)
{
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f4</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-s1">j2</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-s1">f3</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f5</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-en">cos</span>(<span class="pl-s1">f4</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f6</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> (<span class="pl-smi">float</span>)<span class="pl-en">sin</span>(<span class="pl-s1">f4</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">l</span>;
<span class="pl-k">for</span>( <span class="pl-s1">l</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">j2</span>; <span class="pl-s1">l</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-s1">j</span>; <span class="pl-s1">l</span> <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">i2</span>)
{
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">j1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">l</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s1">l1</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f7</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f5</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> (<span class="pl-smi">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">-</span> <span class="pl-s1">f6</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> (<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>]);
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f8</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f5</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> (<span class="pl-smi">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>]) <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-s1">f6</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> (<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>]);
<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">l</span>] <span class="pl-c1">-</span> <span class="pl-s1">f7</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">j1</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">l</span>] <span class="pl-c1">-</span> <span class="pl-s1">f8</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">real</span>[<span class="pl-s1">l</span>] <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f7</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">img</span>[<span class="pl-s1">l</span>] <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">f8</span>;
}

}

<span class="pl-s1">l1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">i2</span>;
<span class="pl-s1">l1</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">i2</span>;


}

<span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>;
}
<span class="pl-c">//**********end FFT **************</span>


<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-en">fftnormalizada</span>(<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">datostimeadc</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>])
{
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">var</span>,<span class="pl-s1">len</span>,<span class="pl-s1">k</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">outstring</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">char</span> <span class="pl-s1">buferr</span>[<span class="pl-c1">10</span>];
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">promedio</span>,<span class="pl-c1">M</span>,<span class="pl-s1">prompos</span>;
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">w</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>];
<span class="pl-smi">float</span> <span class="pl-s1">f</span>[<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>]; 

 <span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>;<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)
      <span class="pl-s1">datostimeadc</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>]<span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-en">sin</span>((<span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-c1">3.1415927</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-c1">60</span>/<span class="pl-c1">512</span>));


 <span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span> <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span> <span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)
   <span class="pl-s1">f</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-c1">FSAMPLE</span>/(<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>);

   <span class="pl-s1">w</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-c1">PI</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-s1">f</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>];
 
 
     
     
     <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
     <span class="pl-s1">promedio</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;
     <span class="pl-k">while</span>( <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>)
       {
	 <span class="pl-c">// al=(2*(al-am)/(aM-am))-1;</span>
	 <span class="pl-c">//al=al*100;</span>
	 <span class="pl-s1">datosrealfft</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] <span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">datostimeadc</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>], <span class="pl-s1">promedio</span> <span class="pl-c1">+=</span> <span class="pl-s1">datostimeadc</span>[<span class="pl-s1">k</span>] ;
	 <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>;
       }
     <span class="pl-c1">M</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">k</span>;
     
     <span class="pl-s1">promedio</span> /= <span class="pl-c1">M</span>;
     <span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">0</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">M</span>; <span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>){

<span class="pl-c">//     fft(-1,MUESTRAS/2,datosrealfft,datosimgfft);</span>
 <span class="pl-en">fft</span>(<span class="pl-c1">-1</span>,<span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>,<span class="pl-s1">datosrealfft</span>,<span class="pl-s1">datosimgfft</span>);
 <span class="pl-k">for</span>(<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>;<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-c1">MUESTRAS</span>;<span class="pl-s1">k</span><span class="pl-c1">++</span>)
